With BullingerDB, we aim to establish a new benchmark for multilingual historical text recognition and temporally-aware writer analysis.

</details>


### [113] [SAM3D-Phys: Towards Multi-Object Interactive Simulation in Real World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30239)
*Xin Dong,Weijian Deng,Lihan Zhang,Tianru Dai,Wenfeng Deng,Yansong Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了SAM3D-Phys框架，结合多视角场景重建与生成式3D先验（SAM3D），从不完整观测中恢复完整且可物理仿真的物体几何，并通过物理约束优化和外观蒸馏确保场景一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有三维重建方法虽能生成视觉逼真的场景，但物体常因遮挡和有限视角而几何不完整，无法用于物理仿真。需要一种方法从部分观测中恢复完整几何，并保持与场景的一致性。

Method: 1) 多视图重建获得场景几何和部分物体观测；2) 利用SAM3D生成式先验推断完整物体几何；3) 通过物理约束空间优化将恢复物体对齐到原始位置；4) 掩码引导的外观蒸馏模块基于观测图像优化纹理保真度。

Result: SAM3D-Phys能够从部分观测中恢复完整物体几何，并还原其在场景中的姿态和外观，生成适合物理仿真的干净物体表征，支持多个物体同时进行一致的物理交互仿真。

Conclusion: 该框架成功弥合了视觉重建与物理仿真之间的鸿沟，通过生成式先验补全物体几何，结合物理约束和外观蒸馏保证场景一致性，使重建场景中的物体具备可交互的物理仿真能力。

Abstract: This work addresses the problem of recovering complete, simulatable object geometry from reconstructed real-world scenes, enabling physics-based interaction with objects embedded in the scene. While modern multi-view reconstruction methods can produce visually accurate environments, objects are often incomplete due to occlusions and limited observations, making them unsuitable for physics simulation. To address this limitation, we propose SAM3D-Phys, a framework that integrates scene reconstruction with generative 3D priors of SAM3D to recover physically simulatable objects. Our approach first reconstructs the scene from multi-view images to obtain scene geometry and partial observations of objects. We then leverage SAM3D to infer complete object geometry from these partial observations. To ensure that the recovered objects remain consistent with the reconstructed scene, we restore scene-consistent object states through two complementary strategies: a physics-constrained spatial optimization algorithm that iteratively aligns the recovered object to its original location, and a mask-guided appearance distillation module that refines texture fidelity based on the observed images. By recovering complete object geometry and restoring its pose and appearance within the scene, SAM3D-Phys produces clean object representations suitable for physics-based simulation, enabling simultaneous and physically consistent interactive simulation of multiple objects within a reconstructed scene. Project page: https://chnxindong.github.io/sam3d-phys/

</details>


### [114] [Reinforcement Learning with Robust Rubric Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30244)
*Ya-Qi Yu,Hao Wang,Fangyu Hong,Xiangyang Qu,Gaojie Wu,Qiaoyu Luo,Nuo Xu,Huixin Wang,Wuheng Xu,Yongxin Liao,Zihao Chen,Haonan Li,Ziming Li,Dezhi Peng,Minghui Liao,Jihao Wu,Haoyu Ren,Dandan Tu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RLR³，将可验证奖励的强化学习从任务级扩展到标准级，通过双路径执行（LLM提取器+确定性验证器、LLM评判者）、最小暴露策略和层次化聚合，有效处理部分可验证的视觉语言任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR仅适用于可确定性检查的任务，但许多视觉语言任务只能部分验证，需要多标准监督（如感知细节、推理步骤、约束条件）。Rubric提供细粒度监督接口，但其在线RL中的执行准确性不足。

Method: 1) 双路径执行：LLM提取器+确定性验证器处理可验证标准，LLM评判者处理不可验证标准；2) 最小暴露策略：对提取器屏蔽真实标注、对评判者屏蔽图像；3) 层次化聚合：优先关键标准，缓解回合组内分数饱和。

Result: 在15个基准上评估Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B，RLR³一致优于RLVR，相比基础模型提升4.7分，超过官方 instruct-to-thinking 模型差距。控制审计证实确定性验证和最小暴露显著减少了可被利用的假阳性。

Conclusion: RLR³通过细粒度、鲁棒的Rubric奖励机制，有效将RLVR扩展到部分可验证的视觉语言任务，显著提升模型性能并减少奖励漏洞。

Abstract: While Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) is effective for deterministically checkable tasks, many vision-language tasks are partially verifiable, demanding multi-criteria supervision (e.g., perceptual details, reasoning steps, and constraints). Rubrics provide a natural interface for this fine-grained supervision, but their effectiveness depends on the execution accuracy during online RL. We propose Reinforcement Learning with Robust Rubric Rewards ($\text{RLR}^3$), extending RLVR from task-level verification to criterion-level verification. $\text{RLR}^3$ routes instance-specific rubrics through two execution paths: an LLM-as-an-extractor paired with a deterministic verifier, or an LLM-as-a-Judge for non-verifiable criteria. To ensure faithful scoring, $\text{RLR}^3$ introduce a minimal exposure strategy that masks ground truths from extractors and images from judges. Furthermore, $\text{RLR}^3$ employs hierarchical aggregation to prioritize essential criteria over additional criteria, and mitigates score saturation within rollout groups. Evaluated on Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B across 15 benchmarks, $\text{RLR}^3$ consistently outperforms RLVR, yielding a 4.7-point improvement over the base model and exceeding the official instruct-to-thinking model gap. Controlled audits confirm our deterministic verification and minimal exposure significantly reduce exploitable false positives.

</details>


### [115] [GenClaw: Code-Driven Agentic Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30248)
*Junyan Ye,Jun He,Zilong Huang,Dongzhi Jiang,Xuan Yang,Rui Chen,Weijia Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GenClaw提出了一种代码驱动的智能体图像生成范式，通过先概念构思、再草图绘制、最后着色三个阶段，结合代码（SVG/HTML/Three.js）作为可控中间画布，将图像生成从黑盒过程转变为类似人类艺术创作的可控、可解释的分阶段流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像生成智能体受限于底层黑盒模型，工作流程陷入反复提示词重写的循环，缺乏直接操作画布的机制，未能充分发挥大语言模型作为精准视觉构建"画笔"的潜力。

Method: GenClaw采用三阶段流程：(1) 通过搜索与推理构建概念知识和上下文；(2) 使用代码（如SVG、HTML、Three.js）渲染可执行的视觉草图；(3) 利用图像生成模型补充纹理、材质和逼真度。代码作为可控中间画布，桥接语言推理与像素合成。

Result: 将图像生成从黑盒范式转变为类似真实人类创作的分阶段过程，实现了高度可控且可解释的视觉生成系统。

Conclusion: GenClaw通过代码驱动的智能体范式，为构建高度可控和可解释的视觉生成系统迈出了一步。

Abstract: Image generation models have evolved from text-conditioned pixel synthesis toward multimodal agents endowed with visual comprehension and tool invocation capabilities. Yet, existing agents remain at the mercy of underlying black-box image models. Their workflow is trapped in a repetitive cycle of prompt rewriting for generation refinement, leaving them with no mechanism to directly manipulate the canvas. In essence, the potential of LLMs to serve as a genuine "brush" for precise visual construction remains largely untapped. In this paper, we propose GenClaw, a code-driven agentic image generation paradigm that empowers the agent to create like a human artist: first conceptualizing, then sketching, and finally coloring. Specifically, the agent first constructs the conceptual knowledge and context through search and reasoning. It then utilizes code (e.g., SVG, HTML, Three.js) to render executable visual sketches. Finally, it employs an image generation model to supplement textures, materials, and photorealism. In this workflow, code serves as a controllable intermediate canvas bridging linguistic reasoning and pixel synthesis, seamlessly integrating programmatic logic with the visual expressiveness of generative models. By transforming image generation from a black-box paradigm into a staged process akin to authentic human creation, GenClaw offers a step toward for highly controllable and interpretable visual generation systems.

</details>


### [116] [Ambient-robust Inverse Rendering using Active RGB-NIR Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30250)
*Hoon-Gyu Chung,Jinnyeong Kim,Hyunwoo Kang,Seung-Hwan Baek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于主动RGB-NIR成像的逆渲染方法，通过近红外闪光照明获得对环境光照鲁棒的几何与反射率重建，并构建了首个多视角RGB-NIR逆渲染数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有逆渲染方法对环境光照敏感，重建结果不准确且易受环境光干扰，需要一种对环境鲁棒的解决方案。

Method: 利用主动RGB-NIR成像，采用近红外闪光（人眼不可见）获取稳定的点光源着色，通过三阶段逆渲染方法融合多视角RGB图像（环境光）和NIR图像（主动闪光），实现几何与反射率的精确重建。

Result: 构建了首个多视角RGB-NIR逆渲染数据集，实验表明该方法在多种环境光照条件下均优于现有方法，实现了准确的几何与反射率估计。

Conclusion: 主动NIR闪光成像能有效解决逆渲染对环境光照敏感的问题，RGB与NIR图像的互补使用可显著提升重建质量。

Abstract: Inverse rendering aims to reconstruct geometry and reflectance of objects from images. Despite recent progress, existing methods often produces inaccurate reconstructions that are sensitive to ambient illumination conditions. Here we introduce an ambient-robust inverse rendering method enabled by active RGB-NIR imaging. Our key insight is to leverage near-infrared (NIR) flash illumination-imperceptible to human observers-to obtain stable point-light shading that is largely invariant to ambient illumination. By using multi-view RGB images illuminated by ambient light and NIR images acquired with active NIR flash illumination, we reconstruct accurate geometry and reflectance by exploiting the complementary benefits of RGB and NIR images via a three-stage inverse rendering method. To enable dense multi-view acquisition, we develop an active imaging system equipped with a RGB-NIR camera and a NIR flash mounted on a mobile base. Using this system, we collect the first multi-view RGB-NIR inverse rendering dataset captured under multiple ambient illumination conditions. Experiments demonstrate that our method outperforms prior approaches, achieving accurate geometry and reflectance estimation across multiple ambient lighting scenarios.

</details>


### [117] [VideoFDB: Evaluating Full-Duplex Vision-Speech Capabilities in Conversational Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30256)
*Amrita Mazumdar,Seonwook Park,Rajarshi Roy,Nikhil Srihari,Shengze Wang,Yuhao Zhou,Julia Wang,Koki Nagano,Shalini De Mello*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VideoFDB是首个全双工视听交互基准，包含237个真实视频对话片段，用于评估多模态对话代理在非语言动态方面的能力。研究发现当前系统存在字幕崩溃、忽视视觉流等问题，且级联语音-虚拟形象系统架构本质上无法产生全双工非语言线索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全双工基准仅评估语音层面，忽略了真实人类对话中视听双通道、同时说听、连续产生非语言线索（点头、微笑、手势等）的完整交互模式，因此需要建立首个评估全双工视听到视听（AV2AV）对话代理的基准。

Method: 构建了237个来自真实视频通话的二人对话片段，覆盖11种非语言对话动态；设计了将感知与生成行为分离的分类体系；提出基于语言模型评估的、具有可解释维度的评估框架。

Result: 开源和闭源视觉-语音代理存在系统性失败模式：字幕坍塌和视觉流忽视；当前系统仅利用视觉进行显式视觉问答，而非自然对话所需的流式联合视听理解；级联语音-虚拟形象系统在架构上无法产生全双工非语言线索。

Conclusion: VideoFDB作为首个全双工AV2AV交互基准，为系统评估奠定了基础，有望加速下一代多模态对话代理的发展。

Abstract: Natural human conversation is full-duplex and audio-visual: people simultaneously speak and listen while continuously interpreting and producing nonverbal cues, such as nods, smiles, and gestures. To support successful human-agent interaction, agents must model full-duplex audiovisual conversation; however, existing full-duplex benchmarks evaluate only speech. In this work, we present VideoFDB, the first benchmark to evaluate full-duplex audio-visual-to-audio-visual (AV2AV) conversational agents. VideoFDB contributes (i) 237 dyadic clips spanning 11 nonverbal conversational dynamics from real-world video calls, (ii) a taxonomy separating perception from generation behaviors, and (iii) a rubric-based LM-as-judge evaluation framework with interpretable axes for assessing conversational quality with respect to nonverbal conversational dynamics. Across open- and closed-source vision-speech agents, we find systematic failure modes: captioning collapse and visual-stream ignorance, and we show that current systems exploit vision for explicit visual question answering but not for the streaming joint audiovisual grounding required in natural conversation. We further evaluate cascaded speech-to-avatar systems and find that their architecture fundamentally precludes the production of full-duplex nonverbal cues. As the first benchmark for full-duplex AV2AV interaction, VideoFDB establishes a foundation for systematic evaluation and, we hope, will accelerate the advancement and development of next-generation multimodal conversational agents.

</details>


### [118] [Stable-Layers: Fine-Tuning Image Layer Decomposition Models with VLM-Scored Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30257)
*Ciara Rowles,Reshinth Adithyan,Nikhil Pinnaparaju,Vikram Voleti,Mark Boss*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Stable-Layers提出无需成对标注的强化学习框架，通过VLM反馈微调层分解模型，解决奖励信号压缩问题，在Crello数据集上取得更好分解效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有层分解模型需要成对标注数据，获取成本高。作者希望利用VLMs的视觉理解能力，通过强化学习实现无需配对监督的微调，同时解决VLM评分方差小、难以提供有效学习信号的问题。

Method: 基于Qwen-Image-Layered模型，采用Flow-GRPO结合LoRA进行微调。关键创新是两阶段评估管道：第一阶段对每个候选分解按五个编辑标准逐样本评分，第二阶段通过网格校准让VLM并排重评所有候选，以增加组内方差，提供有效奖励信号。

Result: 在Crello数据集上，Stable-Layers生成的层分解具有更强的层分离能力，更少的空白或伪影层，以及更低的逐层重建误差，优于基础模型。

Conclusion: 通过精心设计的奖励信号管道，利用VLM反馈进行强化学习可以有效提升层分解质量，为减少配对监督依赖提供可行方案。

Abstract: We present Stable-Layers, a reinforcement learning framework that eliminates the need for paired supervision by fine-tuning a pretrained layer decomposition model using only feedback from a vision-language model (VLM). Starting from Qwen-Image-Layered, we apply Flow-GRPO with LoRA adaptation, sampling multiple candidate decompositions per image, scoring them with a VLM, and optimising the policy from group-relative advantages. The key challenge lies in designing a reliable reward signal: VLMs scoring samples in isolation tend to compress their judgements into a narrow band, leaving GRPO with little within-group variance to learn from. We address this with a two-stage evaluation pipeline that pairs structured per-sample scoring across five edit-centric criteria with a grid-based calibration step in which the VLM re-scores all candidates side-by-side. Stable-Layers produces decompositions with stronger layer separation, fewer blank or artifact-heavy layers, and lower per-layer reconstruction error on the Crello dataset compared to the base model.

</details>


### [119] [minWM: A Full-Stack Open-Source Framework for Real-Time Interactive Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30263)
*Min Zhao,Hongzhou Zhu,Bokai Yan,Zihan Zhou,Yimin Chen,Wenqiang Sun,Kaiwen Zheng,Guande He,Xiao Yang,Chongxuan Li,Fan Bao,Jun Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: minWM是一个全栈开源框架，用于将双向视频扩散基础模型转化为支持相机控制、低延迟、可交互的实时视频世界模型，并提供了完整的数据构建、微调、自回归训练、蒸馏和流式推理流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视频扩散基础模型在高质量视频生成上表现出色，但将其转化为实时交互式视频世界模型仍面临巨大挑战。交互式世界模型需要可控、因果、低延迟的 rollout，这在实践中需要完整的数据构建、可控微调、自回归训练、少步蒸馏和流式推理等全流程支持，亟需一个可复现且可扩展的开源框架。

Method: minWM提供端到端流水线：1) 对双向视频扩散模型进行相机控制微调；2) 通过Causal Forcing/Causal Forcing++流水线（包括自回归扩散训练、因果ODE或因果一致性蒸馏、非对称DMD）将其蒸馏为少步自回归生成器，实现低延迟rollout。框架模块化且架构可扩展，已在Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B和HY1.5-TI2V-8B上实例化，支持交叉注意力条件注入和MMDiT风格架构。

Result: 提供了完整的开源实现，包括可运行脚本、检查点、文档和推理代码，并在相机轨迹质量、可控性训练步数、最小batch-size需求等方面进行了实用消融实验。

Conclusion: minWM作为一套可复现且可扩展的配方，能够有效构建和适配实时交互式视频世界模型，填补了现有视频扩散模型向交互式世界模型转化过程中的全栈工具链空缺。

Abstract: Recent video diffusion foundation models have achieved remarkable progress in high-quality video generation, yet turning them into real-time interactive video world models remains challenging. Interactive world models require controllable, causal, and low-latency rollout, which in practice demands a full pipeline spanning data construction, controllable fine-tuning, autoregressive training, few-step distillation, and streaming inference. In this work, we present minWM, a full-stack open-source framework for building real-time interactive video world models. minWM provides an end-to-end pipeline that converts existing bidirectional T2V/TI2V video foundation models into camera-controllable few-step autoregressive world models. Specifically, minWM first fine-tunes a bidirectional video diffusion model with camera control, and then applies the Causal Forcing / Causal Forcing++ pipeline, including AR diffusion training, causal ODE or causal consistency distillation, and asymmetric DMD, to distill it into a few-step autoregressive generator for low-latency rollout. The framework is modular and architecture-extensible: we instantiate it on representative open backbones, including Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B and HY1.5-TI2V-8B, covering both cross-attention-based condition injection and MMDiT-style architectures. minWM also supports adapting existing video world models, such as HY-WorldPlay, to new data distributions, training recipes, and latency targets. Beyond releasing runnable scripts, checkpoints, documentation, and inference code, we provide practical ablations on camera trajectory quality, controllability training steps, and minimal batch-size requirements. We hope minWM serves as a reproducible and extensible recipe for building and adapting real-time interactive video world models.
  Project Page: [https://github.com/shengshu-ai/minWM](https://github.com/shengshu-ai/minWM)

</details>


### [120] [LoMo: Local Modality Substitution for Deeper Vision-Language Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30265)
*Feng Han,Zhixiong Zhang,Zheming Liang,Yibin Wang,Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现VLMs存在"载体敏感性"问题：文本和图像虽然语义相同，但模型对模态变化非常脆弱。作者提出LoMo方法，通过局部模态替换将文本片段渲染为图像，构建"文本-图像-文本"交错序列，增强跨模态表征一致性，在13个基准上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLMs训练数据中，文本和图像存在不对称的角色分配（文本作为语言查询，图像作为视觉参考），导致模型对不同模态的信息获取产生偏好，无法对齐语义等价内容在文本载体和图像载体上的表征，使得模态替换时推理性能急剧下降。

Method: 提出Local Modality Substitution (LoMo)轻量级数据策展范式：动态选择文本中的目标片段，将其重新渲染为图像，构建"文本-图像-文本"无缝交错的多模态序列，为语义等价的文本和图像载体提供跨模态表征不变性的监督信号。

Result: 在13个多模态基准上，LoMo显著提升多模态推理和跨模态融合能力。在LLaVA-OneVision-1.5-8B上提升2.67分，在Qwen3.5-9B上提升2.82分，优于标准SFT。

Conclusion: LoMo是一种架构无关的轻量级方法，通过简单的数据格式改造就能有效缓解载体敏感性，提升VLMs的跨模态表征一致性，为多模态对齐提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have achieved substantial progress across a wide range of understanding and reasoning tasks, driven by large-scale image-text training aimed at multimodal fusion. Ideally, replacing a textual question with its rendered-image counterpart should leave model performance essentially unaffected. In practice, however, such modality substitution induces dramatic performance degradation. We attribute this "carrier sensitivity" issue to an inherent bias in current training corpora. Across prevalent datasets such as image captioning, VQA, OCR, and web-sourced interleaved data, text and images are typically organized into distinct and asymmetric roles, with text serving as linguistic queries and images as visual references. Such data bias leads VLMs to exhibit distinct preferences for information acquisition across different modalities. Consequently, VLMs fail to align representations of semantically equivalent content across textual and visual carriers, making model reasoning fragile under modality substitution. To address this, we propose Local Modality Substitution (LoMo), a lightweight, architecture-agnostic data curation paradigm designed to provide supervision for cross-modal representational invariance between semantically equivalent text and image carriers. LoMo achieves this by reformulating single-modality prompts into seamlessly interleaved multimodal sequences. It dynamically selects target text spans and recasts them as rendered images, thereby preserving the same semantics across "text, visual, text" carriers. Extensive experiments across 13 diverse multimodal benchmarks demonstrate that LoMo significantly improves overall multimodal reasoning and yields deeper cross-modal fusion. Specifically, it delivers consistent gains across foundational models, improving over standard SFT by 2.67 points on LLaVA-OneVision-1.5-8B and 2.82 points on Qwen3.5-9B.

</details>


### [121] [PhyGenHOI: Physically-Aware 4D Generation of Dynamic Human-Object Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30268)
*Omer Benishu,Gal Fiebelman,Sagie Benaim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhyGenHOI是一个结合生成式人体运动与显式物理物体模拟的框架，通过运动扩散模型控制人体、物质点法模拟物体、3D高斯作为统一表示，并引入窗口化吸引损失、接触驱动重模拟和掩码视频SDS三种机制，实现从静态3D人体和物体生成物理一致且视觉逼真的4D人-物交互动态场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4D人-物交互生成方法缺乏物理一致性，难以生成真实接触和符合物理规律的动态。作者希望耦合生成式运动与显式物理模拟，以同时保证视觉真实感和物理准确性。

Method: 1) 将人体建模为由运动扩散模型驱动的语义智能体，将物体建模为由物质点法模拟的物理智能体，统一使用3D高斯表示；2) 提出窗口化吸引损失实现时间同步；3) 接触驱动重模拟步骤实现动量传递；4) 掩码视频SDS目标增强接触保真度。

Result: 实验表明PhyGenHOI能在多种动作、人体和物体组合下生成物理一致的4D人-物交互，显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 将生成式运动模型与显式物理模拟相结合，通过三种耦合监督机制，可以有效生成既视觉逼真又物理准确的4D人-物交互动态。代码和视频已开源。

Abstract: We address the task of generating physically accurate and visually faithful 4D Human-Object Interaction (HOI). Given a static 3D human and target object represented as 3D Gaussian Splats (3DGS), our goal is to synthesize dynamic scenes where the human actively engages with the object through actions, such as punching or kicking, in accordance with a given input text. To this end, we introduce PhyGenHOI, a novel framework that couples generative human motion with an explicit physical object simulation. We model the human as a semantic agent driven by a Motion Diffusion Model (MDM) and the object as a physical agent simulated via the Material Point Method (MPM), utilizing 3D Gaussians as a unified, differentiable representation. We supervise their interaction through three coupled mechanisms: (1) A Windowed Attraction Loss that temporally synchronizes generative motion to intercept the object; (2) A Contact-Driven Re-simulation step that triggers physically consistent momentum transfer upon impact; and (3) A Masked Video-SDS objective that injects video-based priors to enhance contact fidelity. Experiments show PhyGenHOI generates physically consistent 4D HOI across diverse actions, humans, and objects, outperforming baselines. Project page and videos: https://omerbenishu.github.io/PhyGenHOI/

</details>


### [122] [Boosting Image Quality Assessment Performance: Unsupervised Score Fusion by Deep Maximum a Posteriori Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30269)
*Zhongling Wang,Raymond Zhou,Shahrukh Athar,Wenbo Yang,Zhou Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于深度最大后验估计的无监督IQA分数融合框架，通过细粒度不确定性估计提升融合预测的准确性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单个IQA模型往往对特定图像内容或失真类型存在偏见，因此需要融合多个模型的分数以取长补短，但目前缺乏最优的融合方案。

Method: 利用深度最大后验(MAP)估计进行无监督IQA分数融合，在分数层面进行细粒度的不确定性估计，从而提升融合精度并降低不确定性。

Result: 在多个实验上优于单个IQA模型以及其他融合方法，并展现出在融合过程中自动排斥"坏"模型的能力。

Conclusion: 所提框架能有效融合多个IQA模型，实现更准确、更鲁棒的图像质量评估，且具有模型选择能力。

Abstract: Over the past decades, numerous Image Quality Assessment (IQA) models have emerged, aiming to predict the perceptual quality of images. However, individual models are often biased toward certain types of image content or distortions, depending on the design principle and process. An intuitive idea is to harness the strengths and mitigate the weaknesses of each IQA model, by fusing the scores of multiple models into a stronger one. Here we make one of the first attempts to seek an optimal solution for the idea and propose a general framework for unsupervised IQA score fusion using deep Maximum a Posteriori (MAP) estimation. The proposed model conducts fine-grained uncertainty estimation at the score level to increase the accuracy and reduce the uncertainty in fused predictions. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate the superiority of the proposed model over individual IQA models and other fusion methods. It also exhibits an interesting capability of rejecting ``bad" models in the fusion process.

</details>


### [123] [Grounded 3D-Aware Spatial Vision-Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30307)
*An-Chieh Cheng,Yang Fu,Yatai Ji,Ligeng Zhu,Guanqi Zhan,Zhuoyang Zhang,Zhaojing Yang,Song Han,Yao Lu,Pavlo Molchanov,Vidya Nariyambut Murali,Jan Kautz,Xiaolong Wang,Hongxu Yin,Sifei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GR3D是一种统一显式2D定位、隐式2D定位和单目3D定位能力的空间视觉语言模型，通过生成时插入区域标记的隐式定位机制和基于区域查询的单目3D定位设计，实现了从2D感知到3D推理的空间理解分解，在多项空间基准上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在空间理解中缺乏统一的定位能力，尤其是隐式定位和3D定位的整合不足，难以有效分解复杂的空间推理问题。

Method: 1) 隐式定位机制：在生成空间思维链时识别实体提及并插入区域标记；2) 区域提示的单目3D定位：从区域查询预测相机视角下的3D边界框，辅以内参归一化和密集几何监督；3) 三种定位能力统一在单一框架中。

Result: 在定位和非定位空间基准测试上均取得一致改进，证明定位作为归纳偏置能有效增强空间理解能力。

Conclusion: 定位能力不仅是定位任务本身的有效工具，还能作为归纳偏置提升视觉语言模型的整体空间理解能力。

Abstract: We present GR3D, a spatial vision language model equipped with three complementary grounding capabilities--explicit 2D grounding, implicit 2D grounding, and monocular 3D grounding--within a single framework. GR3D introduces an implicit grounding mechanism that identifies entity mentions during generation and inserts the corresponding region tokens into the text stream, allowing the model to reference visual evidence on the fly when producing spatial chain-of-thought responses. In parallel, a region-prompted monocular 3D grounding design predicts 3D bounding boxes in the camera view from grounded region queries, supported by intrinsic-aware normalization and dense geometric supervision. Together, these grounding capabilities enable GR3D to decompose complex spatial understanding problems into grounded 2D perception followed by 3D inference. GR3D achieves consistent improvements across grounded and non-grounded spatial benchmarks, demonstrating grounding as an effective inductive bias for strengthening spatial understanding in VLMs. These grounding capabilities collectively enhance general spatial understanding beyond the grounding task itself.

</details>


### [124] [City-Mesh3R: Simulation-Ready City-Scale 3D Mesh Reconstruction from Multi-View Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30310)
*Sayan Paul,Sourav Ghosh,Siddharth Katageri,Soumyadip Maity,Sanjana Sinha,Brojeshwar Bhowmick*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: City-Mesh3R提出了一种端到端的可扩展框架，通过分治策略从大规模无序图像集合中直接重建城市级水密三维网格，解决了现有方法在大规模城市场景中几何不完整、表面噪声大及计算复杂的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市场景的三维重建对下游仿真至关重要，但现有基于NeRF、Gaussian Splatting的方法难以恢复可用于仿真的三维网格（几何缺失、表面不规则噪声），而小规模方法扩展到大规模场景计算成本过高。

Method: 采用分治策略：1) 通过拓扑图像聚类、聚类独立稀疏SfM及地图合并构建稀疏城市地图，无需穷尽特征匹配；2) 空间划分后进行几何感知相机选择、密集表面重建及曲率感知自适应顶点密度网格细化；3) 将各分区网格拼接成全局城市网格。

Result: 在城市级重建数据集上的定性和定量结果表明，该方法能生成高保真水密三维网格，具有规则几何结构并捕获精细表面细节。

Conclusion: 该方法具备可扩展性，适用于任意大规模场景的端到端分布式处理。

Abstract: City-scale 3D surface reconstruction from multiview images for downstream 3D simulation, poses highly challenging problems due to the scale and complexity of urban scenes. Existing city-scale 3D reconstruction methods based on NeRF, Gaussian Splatting etc. often fail to recover 3D meshes ready for simulation due to incomplete/missing geometry and irregular, noisy surfaces. Scaling existing small-scale 3D reconstruction methods to arbitrarily large urban scenes is highly infeasible due to their computational complexity. We present City-Mesh3R, a scalable framework for reconstructing watertight surface meshes directly from large unordered image collections. Unlike recent methods which use global sparse SfM point-cloud initialization followed by a distributed 3D dense reconstruction of large-scale scenes, our method follows an end-to-end images-to-mesh 3D reconstruction approach using a divide-and-conquer strategy. The sparse city map is reconstructed via topological image clustering, cluster-wise independent sparse SfM and map merging, without need for exhaustive image feature matching. Then this map is partitioned spatially to perform geometry-aware camera selection, followed by dense surface reconstruction and surface refinement using curvature-aware adaptive vertex density remeshing. These partition meshes are then stitched together to produce the global mesh of the city. The proposed end-to-end framework is evaluated on city-scale reconstruction datasets. As demonstrated by our qualitative and quantitative results, our proposed method yields high-fidelity watertight 3D meshes with regular geometry, capturing fine surface details, and is suitable for scaling to arbitrarily large scenes owing to the end-to-end processing in a distributed setting.

</details>


### [125] [Archon: A Unified Multimodal Model for Holistic Digital Human Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30311)
*Chong Bao,Shichen Liu,Lijun Yu,David Futschik,Stylianos Moschoglou,Shefali Srivastava,Ziqian Bai,Feitong Tan,Guofeng Zhang,Zhaopeng Cui,Sean Fanello,Yinda Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Archon是一个完全预训练的人类中心统一多模态模型，通过模态特定分词器、语义视频重参数化（4倍token缩减）和"模态思维"逐步推理，在文本、音频、动作和视觉等7种模态上实现高保真数字人生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数字人模型难以实现文本、音频、动作、视觉等全模态的统一建模，缺乏一个能处理多模态联合分布的通用框架。

Method: 1) 模态特定分词器统一7种模态；2) 语义视频重参数化实现4倍token压缩；3) 语义驱动视频扩散解码器；4) "Thinking in Modality"策略，将歧义跨模态任务分解为交替模态链的逐步推理。

Result: 在多种数字人生成任务上达到领先或可比性能，验证了统一框架的有效性。

Conclusion: Archon证明了统一预训练的多模态框架在数字人全模态生成中的可行性，为解决token爆炸和高保真视频生成提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Digital humans are fundamental to immersive interaction, yet creating a unified model for holistic modalities, including text, audio, motion, and visual content, remains an open challenge. In this paper, we present Archon, a fully pretrained, human-centric unified multimodal model for holistic avatar generation. Archon unifies seven modalities with modality-specific tokenizers, and a native autoregressive unified multimodal model pretrained on synchronized modalities and 72 diverse tasks to model holistic joint distributions. To address the token explosion challenge in high-fidelity talking videos, we introduce a memory-efficient semantic video reparameterization, achieving 4x token reduction while preserving fine-grained dynamics, coupled with a semantic-driven video diffusion decoder. We further propose a "Thinking in Modality" that decomposes ambiguous cross-modal tasks into stepwise thinking in an alternative chain of modality, progressively enhancing fidelity and controllability. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Archon achieves superior or comparable performance across diverse digital human generation tasks, validating the effectiveness of our unified framework. Project page: https://zju3dv.github.io/archon/.

</details>


### [126] [VPG: Visual Prefix Guidance for Autoregressive Image and Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30317)
*Xinyao Liao,Qiyuan He,Yicong Li,Jiayin Zhu,Xiaoye Qu,Wei Wei,Angela Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Visual Prefix Guidance (VPG)，一种无需训练、推理时引导的方法，通过对比生成前缀和破坏前缀的输出来改善自回归图像和视频生成中的前缀漂移问题，显著提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归图像/视频生成器在训练时使用teacher-forcing，但推理时需基于自身生成的前缀进行采样，导致暴露偏差(Exposure Bias)和前缀漂移(Prefix Drift)问题。现有方法主要依赖外部语义条件（如类别标签或文本提示）进行引导，而非检验下一步预测对已生成前缀的后验支持强度。

Method: VPG是一种无需训练的推理时引导方法。通过对比模型在生成前缀下的输出与在破坏前缀（corrupted prefix）下的输出，外推logits，使得预测朝向能增强生成前缀后验支持的候选方向。

Result: 在VAR的类别条件图像生成、Infinity的文生图和InfinityStar的文生视频任务中，VPG无需重训模型即可提升生成质量。VAR的FID平均降低0.36，图像和视频生成的基准测试性能均得到提升。

Conclusion: VPG通过利用生成前缀自身的后验支持作为引导信号，有效缓解了自回归视觉生成中的前缀漂移问题，是一种通用且高效的训练-free推理时优化方法。

Abstract: Autoregressive image and video generators are trained with teacher-forced histories but must sample from their own generated prefixes at inference time, making them vulnerable to exposure bias and prefix drift. Existing remedies either modify training or apply sampling-time guidance aimed primarily at external semantic conditions, such as class labels or text prompts, rather than testing whether a next-step prediction provides strong posterior support for the generated prefix itself. We propose Visual Prefix Guidance (VPG), a training-free inference-time guidance method for autoregressive image and video generation. VPG improves next-step prediction by contrasting the model's output under the generated prefix with its output under a corrupted prefix, then extrapolating logits toward candidates that strengthen the posterior support of the generated prefix. Across class-conditional image generation with VAR, text-to-image generation with Infinity, and text-to-video generation with InfinityStar, VPG improves generation quality without retraining the base model, reducing FID on VAR by 0.36 on average and improving benchmark performance on both image and video generation.

</details>


### [127] [MonoPhysics: Estimating Geometry, Appearance, and Physical Parameters from Monocular Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30320)
*Daniel Rho,Jun Myeong Choi,Matthew Thornton,Biswadip Dey,Roni Sengupta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MonoPhysics提出了一种从单目视频中联合优化形变物体的几何、外观和物理参数的方法，利用可微MPM模拟和3D高斯泼溅技术，在缺少多视图几何约束的情况下，通过三种视觉-物理桥梁实现了与多视图方法相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的逆物理方法依赖多视图视频中的几何约束来解析尺度和3D结构，而在单目设置中缺乏这些约束，导致严重的尺度歧义、不准确的几何结构，以及外观优化与物理模拟之间的弱耦合。

Method: 提出了MonoPhysics框架，结合可微MPM模拟和3D高斯泼溅，通过三种视觉-物理桥梁（全局尺度对齐、物理感知的几何细化、可微位置图）实现单目观察下的联合优化。

Result: 在Vid2Sim和新数据集（包含弹性和塑性物体）上的实验表明，MonoPhysics在单目设置中优于现有基线，且仅使用单摄像头即可达到与多视图基线相当的性能。

Conclusion: MonoPhysics成功解决了单目逆物理估计中的尺度歧义和几何不准确问题，证明了单目设置下也能实现高质量物理参数估计。

Abstract: Existing inverse physics methods recover physical parameters from multi-view videos, where geometric constraints across views resolve scale and 3D structure. In monocular settings, however, such constraints are absent, leading to severe scale ambiguity, inaccurate geometry, and weak coupling between appearance optimization and physical simulation. We propose MonoPhysics, a framework for monocular inverse physics estimation of deformable objects using differentiable MPM simulation and 3D Gaussian Splatting, which jointly optimizes geometry, appearance, and physical parameters from a single camera view. We address these challenges through three visual-physical bridges: global scale alignment, physics-aware geometry refinement, and a differentiable position map, which together enable accurate optimization from monocular observations alone. We evaluate on Vid2Sim and our new dataset of elastic and plastic objects, showing that MonoPhysics outperforms existing baselines in monocular settings and achieves performance comparable to multi-view baselines using only a single camera. Our project page is available at https://daniel03c1.github.io/MonoPhysics/

</details>


### [128] [Veda: Scalable Video Diffusion via Distilled Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30325)
*Shihao Han,Hao Yang,Xinting Hu,Xiaofeng Mei,Yi Jiang,Xiaojuan Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Veda提出了一种蒸馏稀疏注意力框架，通过基于瓦片几何的对齐和硬件高效的内核，在长视频扩散模型中实现了5.1倍端到端加速，且无明显质量下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散Transformer在生成长视频时，自注意力的计算复杂度随序列长度呈二次增长，现有稀疏注意力方法在高稀疏率下效果显著退化。

Method: Veda将瓦片选择建模为从全注意力显式重构的问题，包含统计感知的瓦片评分和头感知瓦片化以减少估计误差与结构不匹配，并设计了硬件高效的瓦片跳跃内核。

Result: 在Waver和Wan2.1等模型上，生成720P 10秒视频时实现5.1倍端到端加速和10.5倍自注意力加速，注意力开销从92%降至50%，且加速比随序列长度增加而提升。

Conclusion: 生成质量的关键不在于稀疏率本身，而在于稀疏掩码能否与全注意力的瓦片级几何结构对齐，Veda通过蒸馏方法实现了这一对齐，从而在极高稀疏率下仍保持生成质量。

Abstract: Scaling Diffusion Transformers to generate high-resolution, long videos is constrained by the quadratic cost of self-attention, and existing sparse attention methods degrade under high sparsity. We show empirically that generation quality is determined not by the sparsity ratio itself, but by how well the sparse mask aligns with the tile-wise geometry of full attention. Based on this insight, we propose Veda, a distilled sparse attention framework that formulates tile selection as an explicit reconstruction problem from full attention. Veda integrates statistics-aware tile scoring with head-aware tiling to reduce estimation error and structural mismatch, enabling aggressive sparsity. A hardware-efficient tile-skipping kernel converts theoretical sparsity into practical wall-clock speedups. Experiments on large video diffusion models, including Waver and Wan2.1, demonstrate substantial acceleration with no noticeable degradation in generation quality. To generate 720P 10-second videos on Waver-T2V-12B, Veda achieves a 5.1$\times$ end-to-end speedup and a 10.5$\times$ self-attention speedup, reducing attention overhead from 92% to 50%. Notably, the gains increase with sequence length, indicating that Veda scales favorably with spatiotemporal resolution across models.

</details>


### [129] [Supercharging Thermal Gaussian Splatting with Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30328)
*Manoj Biswanath,Chenxin Cai,Hannah Schieber,Daniel Roth,Benjamin Busam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种仅使用热成像图像和深度估计的3D场景表示方法 Thermal-to-Depth Gaussian Splatting (TDg)，无需多模态数据融合，在保持较高渲染质量的同时显著缩短了训练时间（减少55%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态3D场景表示方法（如3D高斯泼溅）虽然性能提升，但多模态数据融合会导致处理速度变慢并引入额外挑战。作者希望摆脱对可见光的依赖，仅使用单模态（热红外）来实现更高效、更鲁棒的3D场景重建。

Method: 提出TDg方法，仅使用热红外图像和深度估计构建辐射场，无需多模态数据融合，利用单模态架构实现3D场景表示。

Result: 在RGBT-Scenes和ThermalMix数据集上，TDg在LPIPS、SSIM、PSNR上分别比基线MSMG提升1.12%、0.034%、0.01%，训练时间减少12分47秒（提升55%）。

Conclusion: TDg成功实现了基于热红外的辐射场重建，在效率上具有显著优势，可应用于热源检测、搜救、工业监测等领域。

Abstract: Efficient and robust 3D scene representation is crucial in autonomous driving, robotics, and related fields. While RGB images provide valuable content for 3D reconstruction, other modalities like thermal or depth can enable additional information on the environment. Lately, novel view synthesis methods like 3D Gaussian Splatting have started using multiple modalities to further boost their performance. But fusing or combining multimodal data can make the process slower and can bring in additional challenges. Therefore, our project aims to use single modality based on thermal infrared domain, by removing the reliance on visible light as much as possible. This single modality can be expected to be faster as it does not rely on multimodal data. We propose a method, Thermal-to-Depth Gaussian Splatting (TDg), that uses only thermal images and depth estimation in its architecture to derive the radiance fields. Our TDg method outperforms the MSMG (Multiple Single-Modal Gaussians) baseline in most cases on our test datasets, RGBT-Scenes and ThermalMix. On average, the rendering quality metrics such as learned perceptual image patch similarity (LPIPS), structural similarity index measure (SSIM), and peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR) of TDg are 1.12%, 0.034%, and 0.01% better than the baseline MSMG values. It also reduces the training time significantly, by 12 mins 47 secs (55% improvement). Overall, our method is successful in deriving these thermal radiance fields, which can ultimately have several applications, such as identifying heat sources critical in surveillance, search or rescue operations, and industrial inspections where temperature is widely used to monitor machines.

</details>


### [130] [Colored Noise Diffusion Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30332)
*Hadar Davidson,Noam Issachar,Sagie Benaim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Colored Noise Sampling (CNS)，一种无训练、即插即用的扩散模型随机求解器。它利用扩散模型的频谱偏置（先低频后高频），通过动态、时步与频率相关的噪声注入策略替代传统白噪声，更高效地将能量分配到未解决的频带，显著提升图像生成质量。在ImageNet-256上，SiT-XL/2的FID从8.26降至6.27。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SDE求解器无视扩散模型生成轨迹的频谱偏置（早期解决低频结构，后期解决高频细节），在整个过程中均匀注入白噪声，浪费有限能量预算。作者希望建立一种考虑频谱偏置的数学框架，实现更高效的频率解耦能量转移。

Method: 1. 建立将SDE推理重新理解为定向、频率解耦能量转移的数学框架。2. 提出Colored Noise Sampling (CNS)，一种无训练的随机求解器。3. CNS使用动态的、与时步和频率相关的噪声调度，而非均匀白噪声，将注入能量主动导向尚未解决的频带，利用模型的固有频谱偏置引导生成分布向真实数据流形靠近。

Result: 在ImageNet-256上，无引导FID显著降低：SiT-XL/2从8.26到6.27，JiT-B/16从32.39到26.69，JiT-H/16从11.88到8.31。在有Classifier-Free Guidance条件下也保持一致的相对FID提升。

Conclusion: CNS作为一种即插即用的推理时采样器替代方案，在多种架构（SiT, JiT, FLUX）上显著优于标准ODE和SDE基线，验证了利用频谱偏置进行定向噪声注入的有效性。

Abstract: Diffusion models achieve state-of-the-art image synthesis, with their generative trajectories fundamentally exhibiting a spectral bias, resolving low-frequency global structures early and high-frequency fine details later. Conventional stochastic differential equation (SDE) solvers fail to account for this dynamic, naively injecting uniform white noise throughout the entire process and misusing the finite energy budget. In this work, we establish a mathematical framework that reconsiders SDE inference as a targeted, frequency-decoupled energy transfer. Leveraging this framework, we introduce Colored Noise Sampling (CNS), a novel, training-free stochastic solver. Rather than injecting uniform white noise, CNS utilizes a dynamic, timestep- and frequency-dependent schedule that more efficiently allocates injected energy toward structurally unresolved frequency bands. By actively exploiting the model's inherent spectral bias, CNS systematically steers the generated distribution toward the true data manifold. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CNS significantly outperforms standard ODE and SDE baselines as a strictly plug-and-play, inference-time sampler substitution across diverse architectures (SiT, JiT, FLUX). Compared to standard sampling on ImageNet-256, CNS achieves substantial unguided FID reductions, improving from 8.26 to 6.27 on SiT-XL/2, 32.39 to 26.69 on JiT-B/16, and 11.88 to 8.31 on JiT-H/16, while yielding consistent relative FID improvements with Classifier-Free Guidance. Project page is available at https://hadardavidson.github.io/CNS/.

</details>


### [131] [REST3D: Reconstructing Physically Stable 3D Scenes from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30338)
*Xiaoxuan Ma,Jiashun Wang,Nicolas Ugrinovic,Yehonathan Litman,Kris Kitani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: REST3D提出了一种从单张RGB图像重建物理稳定3D场景的框架，通过物理场景理解与物理约束优化相结合，显著减少物理错误并提升模拟稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单图像3D重建方法虽能生成几何合理的场景，但常出现物体悬浮、穿透等物理不一致问题，导致物理模拟不稳定。而基于图像条件的场景生成方法虽改善了物理合理性，却依赖强场景先验，物体布局与输入图像不够匹配。

Method: 1) 提出智能物理场景理解技术，从重力-支撑视角构建场景树（scene-tree），捕获物体物理状态与物体间关系作为结构先验；2) 利用场景树指导，使用图像到3D模型进行场景初始化，再通过场景树引导的对齐和物理约束优化，在解决物理违规的同时保持与输入图像的视觉一致性。

Result: 在合成与真实数据集上，该方法显著减少了物理错误，提升了模拟稳定性，同时保持了良好的重建质量；并在VR人机交互中展示了其沉浸式应用潜力。

Conclusion: REST3D成功将物理理解融入单图像3D重建流程，在保持视觉一致性的同时实现了物理稳定的场景重建，为沉浸式交互和内容创作提供了可行的方案。

Abstract: Reconstructing physically stable 3D scenes from a single RGB image enables casual images to be converted into simulation-ready digital assets for applications such as immersive interaction and content creation. However, existing single-image reconstruction methods fall short in capturing the physical structure of a scene. As a result, they often produce geometrically plausible but physically inconsistent results, including object floating and penetration, which lead to unstable behavior in physics simulations. Image-conditioned scene generation methods improve physical plausibility but often rely on strong scene priors, yielding plausible yet inaccurate object arrangements that fail to match the input image. We propose REST3D, a single-image reconstruction framework that can reconstruct physically stable 3D scenes by integrating physical scene understanding with physics-constrained refinement. We first introduce an agentic physical scene understanding technique that constructs a scene-tree representation capturing object physical states and inter-object relationships from a gravity-support perspective, providing a structural prior for reconstruction. Leveraging this structure, we initialize the scene using image-to-3D models, followed by scene-tree-guided alignment and physics-constrained optimization to resolve physical violations while preserving visual consistency with the input image. Experiments show that our method significantly reduces physical errors and improves simulation stability on both synthetic and real-world datasets while maintaining strong reconstruction quality. We further demonstrate the reconstructed scenes in VR-based human-object interaction, showing their potential for immersive applications.

</details>


### [132] [Benchmarking Single-Factor Physical Video-to-Audio Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30339)
*Tingle Li,Siddharth Gururani,Kevin J. Shih,Gantavya Bhatt,Sang-gil Lee,Zhifeng Kong,Arushi Goel,Gopala Anumanchipalli,Ming-Yu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlatSounds 是一个用于评估视频到音频生成模型物理推理能力的基准。研究发现现有模型依赖文本描述而非视觉信息来推断物理属性，且文本描述虽提升物理和语义准确性，但会损害时间对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频到音频生成模型虽能产生逼真音效，但缺乏对生成音频物理正确性的评估，忽略了对模型物理推理能力的检验。

Method: 构建FlatSounds基准，包含两类测试：1) 受控反事实对（单物理因素变化），2) 单视频模式测试（内部一致性和方向趋势检验）。评估多种SOTA模型。

Result: 模型依赖文本描述而非视觉流来推断物理和语义；文本描述提升物理和语义准确性，但反而降低时间对齐；物理指标与人类偏好高度相关。

Conclusion: 需要超越音频质量评估，推动模型直接从像素学习物理过程，而非依赖文本先验。

Abstract: Generative video-to-audio (V2A) models produce highly plausible soundtracks, but it remains unclear whether they capture the underlying physical processes. Existing evaluations emphasize perceptual realism and overlook physical correctness under controlled interventions. In this paper, we introduce FlatSounds, a benchmark that audits the physical reasoning of V2A models through: 1) controlled counterfactual pairs in which a single physical factor is varied, and 2) single-video pattern tests that probe internal consistency and directional trends. These settings test whether the generated audio correctly reflects specific physical properties and timings. Our evaluation of state-of-the-art models reveals a consistent trade-off: models rely more on text captions than the visual stream to infer physics and semantics. Captions generally improve physical and semantic accuracy, but paradoxically degrade temporal alignment. Our results highlight the need to move beyond audio quality toward learning physical processes directly from pixels. Finally, we find that our physics-based metrics correlate strongly with human preference tests on our own data. Project webpage: https://research.nvidia.com/labs/cosmos-lab/flatsounds/

</details>


### [133] [GPIC: A Giant Permissive Image Corpus for Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30341)
*Keshigeyan Chandrasegaran,Kyle Sargent,Suchir Agarwal,Michael Jang,Michael Poli,Juan Carlos Niebles,Justin Johnson,Jiajun Wu,Li Fei-Fei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GPIC是一个约28万亿像素的巨型许可图像数据集，包含100M训练样本、200K验证样本和1M测试样本，所有图像均采用许可协议、经过安全过滤和去重处理，并提供生成式建模的基准测试协议和像素空间流匹配的参考基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉生成建模的可扩展方法需要大规模、可访问且稳定的数据集，现有数据集在许可、规模或可访问性方面存在不足。

Method: 收集约28万亿像素的互联网图像，使用先进视觉语言模型进行字幕标注，进行安全过滤和去重，在Hugging Face上集中托管，并提供基准测试协议和像素空间流匹配基线。

Result: 构建了GPIC数据集（100M训练/200K验证/1M测试），所有图像采用许可协议，并提供完整的评估工具包和代码。

Conclusion: GPIC为视觉生成建模研究提供了大规模、许可友好、易于访问的数据集和基准，有助于推动该领域的可扩展方法研究。

Abstract: Studying scalable methods for visual generative modeling requires large, accessible, and stable datasets. We introduce GPIC, a Giant Permissive Image Corpus of approximately 28 trillion pixels. GPIC comprises diverse internet images captioned by a state-of-the-art vision-language model, including 100M training, 200K validation, and 1M test examples. Moreover, all GPIC images are permissively licensed for both research and commercial use. GPIC is safety-filtered, deduplicated, and centrally hosted on Hugging Face. We provide a benchmarking protocol for generative modeling on GPIC. Finally, we provide a reference baseline for pixel-space flow matching on GPIC. Our dataset, benchmark, and models are available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/stanford-vision-lab/gpic. Evaluation toolkit and code are available at https://gpic.stanford.edu

</details>


### [134] [YoCausal: How Far is Video Generation from World Model? A Causality Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30346)
*You-Zhe Xie,Yu-Hsuan Li,Jie-Ying Lee,Kaipeng Zhang,Yu-Lun Liu,Zhixiang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出YoCausal基准，通过反转真实视频构建反事实样本，评估视频扩散模型对因果关系的理解，发现感知时间箭头不等于理解因果关系，且与人类水平存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多依赖合成数据，存在模拟到真实的鸿沟，无法评估VDM在真实场景中的因果理解能力，因此需要构建基于真实世界的因果推理评估方法。

Method: 基于认知科学中的违预期范式，通过零成本时间反转真实视频构建自然反事实样本。Level 1用反向惊喜指数（RSI）量化时间箭头感知，Level 2用因果认知指数（CCI）结合VLM将数据集分层为因果与非因果子集，分离真正的因果推理与时间偏差。

Result: 评估13个SOTA VDM发现：感知时间箭头不等于理解因果关系，VDM的因果认知能力与人类水平仍有显著差距。

Conclusion: 当前视频扩散模型虽能捕捉时间统计模式，但缺乏真正的因果推理能力，需进一步发展因果感知的视频生成模型。

Abstract: As video diffusion models (VDMs) advance toward world models, a key question arises: do they truly understand causality, or merely overfit to statistical temporal patterns? Existing benchmarks mostly rely on synthetic data, limiting real-world generalization due to the sim-to-real gap. We present YoCausal, a two-level benchmark inspired by the Violation of Expectation (VoE) paradigm from cognitive science. By temporally reversing real-world videos at zero cost as natural counterfactual samples, YoCausal establishes an arbitrarily extensible evaluation protocol. Level 1 introduces the Reverse Surprise Index (RSI), quantifying arrow-of-time perception via denoising loss. Level 2 introduces the Causality Cognition Index (CCI), which leverages a VLM to stratify datasets into causal and non-causal subsets, disentangling genuine causal reasoning from temporal bias. Evaluation of 13 state-of-the-art VDMs reveals that perceiving the arrow of time does not imply understanding causality, and a significant gap persists relative to human-level causal cognition.

</details>


### [135] [NeuROK: Generative 4D Neural Object Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30347)
*Chen Geng,Guangzhao He,Yue Gao,Yunzhi Zhang,Shangzhe Wu,Jiajun Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出NeuROK，通过学习物体运动学状态的潜在参数化表示，将4D动态生成简化为低维潜在空间中的动力学问题，在多样化动态物体上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4D动态生成方法依赖预定义的物理模型和系统辨识，限制了其适用类别和数据集规模，缺乏通用且可扩展的数据驱动方案。

Method: 提出NeuROK框架：1）学习一个潜在空间编码物体所有可能状态；2）训练解码器将潜在向量映射为合理变形形状；3）基于Transformer的编码器-解码器架构在大规模4D数据集上训练；4）利用拉格朗日力学将潜在空间中的动力学建模简化为低维问题。

Result: 在多种动态物体类型上验证了该神经模拟框架的有效性和通用性，显著优于先前工作。

Conclusion: 数据驱动的运动学状态参数化方法（NeuROK）能够克服传统物理模拟的局限性，为构建通用的4D世界模型提供了新范式。

Abstract: Data-driven approaches have revolutionized 3D vision, enabling transformers to effectively reconstruct and generate static 3D objects. However, generating simulative 4D dynamics -- realistic temporal deformations of static objects under various physical conditions -- remains challenging and often ad hoc, despite its importance in building comprehensive 3D world models. Most existing methods assume a predefined physical model and use system identification to estimate parameters, restricting these methods to specific categories and small-scale datasets. We propose that these restrictions can be overcome by learning a data-driven kinematic state parameterization for object-centric physical systems. Specifically, we learn both a latent space representing all possible states of the object and a decoder that maps any sampled latent to a plausibly deformed shape of the object. We refer to this parameterization as Neural Object Kinematics (NeuROK), and learn a transformer-based encoder-decoder model on a curated large-scale 4D dataset. This formulation and the learned model significantly simplify the generation of simulative dynamics since we only need to consider the dynamics within a low-dimensional latent space from the Lagrangian mechanics' perspective in classical physics. We demonstrate the effectiveness and generality of this neural simulation framework across diverse dynamic object types, showing clear advantages over prior works. Project page: https://chen-geng.com/neurok

</details>


### [136] [AdaState: Self-Evolving Anchors for Streaming Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30349)
*Yusuf Dalva,Pinar Yanardag*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 自回归视频扩散模型因锚定于首帧导致动态不足，本文提出用自适应隐状态替代静态锚点，通过去噪过程生成随内容演变的场景参考，显著提升视频运动丰富度和场景自然演进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归视频扩散模型结构上锚定于首帧，其KV表示在注意力缓存中占据特权位置，导致模型过度关注静态场景一致性，抑制了视频动态、相机运动和场景演进。

Method: 用一个可自适应演变的隐状态替代静态首帧锚点：该状态不参与渲染，随每块内容一同去噪，通过同时关注前一状态和当前内容生成新的场景参考。时间变为相对性，每步生成结构相同，状态转换一致，在去噪过程中自然形成循环。

Result: 实验表明自适应状态显著提升了视频动态，生成更丰富的运动和自然的场景演进。

Conclusion: 通过将静态锚点替换为自适应隐状态，并在去噪过程中引入循环机制，有效解除了首帧对视频动态的抑制，实现了更自然的视频生成。

Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion models generate streaming video by producing frames sequentially, conditioning each chunk on previously generated content. These models are structurally anchored to the first frame: its key-value representation occupies a privileged position in the attention cache and serves as the primary scene reference throughout generation. As the cleanest and most error-free position in the cache, this anchor draws disproportionate attention, suppressing video dynamics, and locking scene composition to the initial viewpoint even as the scene naturally evolves. The result is a temporally shallow video in which motion, camera movement, and scene progression are dampened in favor of static consistency. To address this, we replace the static anchor with an adaptive state, a hidden latent that the model denoises alongside content at every chunk but never renders. Rather than referencing a frozen first frame, the model generates its own scene anchor at each step by attending to both the previous state and the current content, producing a reference that evolves with the generated content. Unlike standard video generation, which encodes an absolute notion of time, our formulation treats time as relative: every generation step sees the same positional structure regardless of how far generation has progressed, and the state transition is identical at every chunk. Together, these properties introduce a recurrence into the generation process, where denoising serves as the transition function, and the KV cache serves as the carrier, requiring no external module. Experiments demonstrate that the adaptive state substantially improves video dynamics, enabling richer motion and natural scene progression within generated videos.

</details>


### [137] [VideoMLA: Low-Rank Latent KV Cache for Minute-Scale Autoregressive Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30351)
*Hidir Yesiltepe,Jiazhen Hu,Tuna Han Salih Meral,Adil Kaan Akan,Kaan Oktay,Hoda Eldardiry,Pinar Yanardag*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次将多头部潜在注意力（MLA）应用于视频扩散模型，通过共享低秩内容潜变量和分离的3D-RoPE位置键，将每token的KV缓存内存减少92.7%，在长视频生成中匹配或超越基线性能，并实现1.23倍吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长视频扩散模型依赖固定大小的滑动窗口KV缓存，但每头KV布局本身（对内存和延迟影响最大）几乎未被改进。作者希望探索MLA在视频扩散中的适用性，以大幅降低KV缓存开销。

Method: 将每头的键和值替换为共享的低秩内容潜变量和共享的分离式3D-RoPE位置键，从而大幅减少每token的KV存储。同时分析了视频注意力并非低秩，但MLA瓶颈决定有效秩而非预训练谱。

Result: 每层缓存中每token的KV内存减少92.7%；在VBench上匹配短时基线的同时，长时基线上取得最佳综合得分；在单张B200上吞吐量提升1.23倍。

Conclusion: MLA在视频扩散中成功并不依赖低秩假设，其瓶颈结构决定了有效秩；该方法在保持生成质量的同时显著降低了内存占用和延迟，适合长视频流式生成。

Abstract: Long-rollout causal video diffusion has converged on a fixed-size sliding-window KV cache, with recent progress innovating within this layout by changing which tokens occupy the window or how their positions are encoded. The per-head KV layout itself, a dominant contributor to streaming memory and latency, has been mostly left unchanged. In this paper, we present the first study of Multi-Head Latent Attention (MLA) in video diffusion. VideoMLA replaces per-head keys and values with a shared low-rank content latent and a shared decoupled 3D-RoPE positional key, reducing per-token KV memory by 92.7% at every cached layer. We further investigate why MLA succeeds in video diffusion even though the spectral assumption often used to motivate it in language models does not hold: pretrained video attention is not low-rank, with 99%-energy effective rank far above any practical latent dimension. VideoMLA retains quality at compression ratios where direct spectral approximation would predict large reconstruction error. We show that the MLA bottleneck, rather than the pretrained spectrum, determines the effective rank: both spectral and random initialization occupy nearly the full rank budget from initialization, and training preserves this budget while adapting within it. On VBench, VideoMLA matches short-horizon streaming video diffusion baselines, achieves the best overall score at long horizons among evaluated methods, and improves throughput by 1.23x on a single B200.

</details>


### [138] [GMOS: Grounding Moving Object Segmentation in 3D Space and Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30352)
*Junyu Xie,Tengda Han,Weidi Xie,Andrew Zisserman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GMOS框架，直接在RGB视频上实现3D感知、时间粒度的运动目标分割，解决了传统方法依赖2D辅助模态和忽略瞬时运动状态的问题。同时构建了GMOS-2K数据集和MOS-I评估协议，在多个基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MOS方法存在两个根本局限：1）依赖预计算的2D辅助模态（如光流、点轨迹），缺乏3D几何信息；2）将运动视为序列级属性，忽略了每个目标的瞬时运动状态。本文旨在解决这两个问题。

Method: 提出GMOS框架，直接在RGB视频上进行3D感知、时间细粒度的多运动目标分割；另提供前背景变体GMOS-S以加速部署。构建GMOS-2K数据集（2,210个真实视频，含逐目标时间运动标注），并形式化MOS-I评估协议及三项互补指标。

Result: 在MOS、MOS-I和无监督VOS基准上达到SOTA结果，运行速度显著快于先前的多目标MOS方法，支持在线推理流式部署。

Conclusion: 将运动目标分割从2D序列级提升到3D瞬时级是有效的，GMOS在精度和速度上均优于现有方法，且具备在线部署能力。

Abstract: Moving Object Segmentation (MOS) aims to discover, segment, and track objects that move independently of the camera. Current MOS methods, however, exhibit two fundamental limitations: they rely on pre-computed 2D auxiliary modalities such as optical flow or point trajectories that lack 3D geometric information, and they treat motion as a sequence-level attribute, overlooking the instantaneous motion state of each object. We address both by grounding MOS in 3D space and time, and propose GMOS, a framework that operates directly on RGB video to produce 3D-aware, temporally fine-grained segmentation of multiple moving objects, alongside a foreground--background variant GMOS-S for faster deployment. To support training and evaluation in this regime, we curate GMOS-2K, a dataset of 2,210 real-world videos with per-object temporal motion annotations drawn from five established Video Object Segmentation (VOS) benchmarks, and formalise MOS-I ("I" for instantaneous), a temporally fine-grained evaluation protocol with three complementary metrics. GMOS achieves state-of-the-art results across MOS, MOS-I, and Unsupervised VOS benchmarks, while running significantly faster than prior multi-object MOS methods and supporting online inference for streaming deployment.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [139] [Human-in-the-Loop Swarms: A Bionic Swarm Approach to Real-World Soil Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29091)
*Petras Swissler,Mohammadali Rashidioun,Nicholas Sahu,Raaid Kabir,Ayodeji Aderibigbe,Oladoyin Kolawole*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"Bionic Swarm"系统，通过人类介入的方式降低群体机器人和现场机器人实验验证的门槛，并在该平台上验证了Score-Biased-Search算法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群体机器人和现场机器人领域因硬件部署成本高、开发时间长，导致从仿真到真实世界验证存在巨大障碍。

Method: 提出Bionic Swarm系统，将难以在机器人上实现但与算法评估无关的任务抽象出来，交由人类用户通过手机Web应用完成；人类通过蓝牙传感器接收指令，集中式服务器运行群体算法并指导人类行动。同时提出了Score-Biased-Search算法，通过对地图位置评分来偏置搜索模式。

Result: 该算法在仿真中展现出超线性的地图重建能力（随搜索代理数量增加）；在Bionic Swarm平台上进行了真实户外实验验证。

Conclusion: 这种人机循环（human-in-the-loop）方法显著降低了现场和群体机器人研究的准入门槛。

Abstract: Swarm and field robotics face significant barriers to real-world validation due to the high cost and development time to deploy hardware. This paper introduces the ``Bionic Swarm,'' a novel system that lowers these barriers by abstracting away many of the tasks that are difficult to implement on robots but which do not contribute to the overall algorithm evaluation, giving these tasks to human users. These human users take directions from a smartphone web-app that takes measurements from Bluetooth-connected sensors and relays them to a centralized server. This server runs the swarm algorithm and directs actions to the human users. We evaluate this system through the experimental validation of a geotechnically-focused search algorithm named Score-Biased-Search, which functions by assigning a ``score'' to each location on a reconstructed map, then biases search patterns through areas of higher expected scores, and which exhibits superlinear map reconstruction relative to the number of search agents. After presenting simulation results for the algorithm, we then apply the algorithm on the Bionic Swarm platform to validate its function in a real-world, outdoor setting. This work demonstrates that this human-in-the-loop approach significantly lowers the barrier to entry for field and swarm robotics research.

</details>


### [140] [Multi-Resolution End-to-End Deep Neural Network for Optimizing Latency-Accuracy Tradeoff in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29138)
*Qitao Weng,Heechul Yun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多分辨率端到端CNN，通过逐分辨率批归一化实现运行时输入尺度选择，在延迟预算下优化自动驾驶的安全性与延迟权衡，在CARLA实验中相比固定分辨率基线持续改善安全指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察到(1)考虑延迟时，最优网络配置随场景上下文和计算资源动态变化；(2)固定分辨率模型在不同条件下会变得次优。因此需要一种能适应延迟预算的动态分辨率选择方法。

Method: 提出多分辨率端到端CNN，通过逐分辨率批归一化支持运行时选择理想输入尺度，并实现分辨率重定向（resolution retargeting）技术，无需原始训练集即可进行多分辨率训练。

Result: 在CARLA城市驾驶挑战中，相比固定分辨率基线，在车道入侵、闯红灯和碰撞等每路线安全性指标上持续取得改进。

Conclusion: 多分辨率方法能有效探索延迟-安全性前沿，根据实时延迟预算动态调整输入分辨率，从而在自动驾驶场景中获得更好的安全性与延迟折中。

Abstract: Latency-accuracy tradeoffs are fundamental in real-time applications of deep neural networks (DNNs) for cyber-physical systems. In autonomous driving, in particular, safety depends on both prediction quality and the end-to-end delay from sensing to actuation. We observe that (1) when latency is accounted for, the latency-optimal network configuration varies with scene context and compute availability; and (2) a single fixed-resolution model becomes suboptimal as conditions change.
  We present a multi-resolution, end-to-end deep neural network for the CARLA urban driving challenge using monocular camera input. Our approach employs a convolutional neural network (CNN) that supports multiple input resolutions through per-resolution batch normalization, enabling runtime selection of an ideal input scale under a latency budget, as well as resolution retargeting, which allows multi-resolution training without access to the original training dataset.
  We implement and evaluate our multi-resolution end-to-end CNN in CARLA to explore the latency-safety frontier. Results show consistent improvements in per-route safety metrics - lane invasions, red-light infractions, and collisions - relative to fixed-resolution baselines.

</details>


### [141] [Learning and Adaptation in Wire Arc Additive Manufacturing Bead Geometry Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29144)
*Chen-Lung Lu,John Wen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于循环神经网络（RNN）和一步超前预测控制的数据驱动方法，用于机器人电弧增材制造（WAAM）的焊道规划与控制。通过在线更新模型（利用前一层预测误差），有效应对热条件变化，显著提升了焊道高度和宽度的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WAAM过程具有复杂非线性的热-几何耦合动力学特性，传统恒定输入或静态模型难以保证焊道高度和宽度的一致性，亟需一种能够适应热条件动态变化的数据驱动控制方法。

Method: 以焊接速度和送丝速度为输入、焊道高度和宽度为输出，采用RNN对输入/输出数据进行建模；结合一步超前预测控制进行焊道规划；通过前一层预测误差更新模型以适应热条件变化。

Result: 在集成线扫描仪反馈的机器人WAAM实验平台上，所提方法在高度和宽度一致性上相比恒定输入和静态模型基线均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 该学习与自适应框架为增材制造过程的鲁棒数据驱动调控提供了一条实用途径。

Abstract: Robotics Wire Arc Additive Manufacturing (WAAM) is governed by complex and nonlinear process dynamics coupling thermal field to the build geometry. The process may be regarded as a multi-input/multi-output dynamical system with welding torch speed and wire feed rate as inputs and weld bead deposition height and width as outputs. In this paper, we use the input/output data to learn a data-driven model and use it for weld planning and control. We show that a simple recurrent neural network architecture and one-step-ahead predictive control can improve the process performance in terms of height and width consistency. To account for the changing thermal conditions during the printing process, we update the learning model using prediction error from the previous layer. This adaptation step further improves the prediction accuracy and controller performance. Experiments on a robotic WAAM testbed with integrated line-scanner feedback significant improvements in height and width consistency compared to constant input and static model baselines. The proposed learning and adaptation framework provides a practical pathway toward robust, data-driven regulation of additive manufacturing processes.

</details>


### [142] [CA-AC-MPC: CUDA-Accelerated Actor-Critic Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29155)
*Antoonio Buo,Vittorio Cammarota,Michele Avagnale,Pierluigi Arpenti,Vincenzo Lippiello,Fabio Ruggiero*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种CUDA加速的actor-critic模型预测控制（AC-MPC）方法，通过在GPU上并行化可微MPC层的前向和反向传播，显著减少了训练和推理延迟，同时在无人机竞速任务中保持了与基线相当的最优控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AC-MPC方法中可微MPC层需要在前向和反向传播中反复求解优化问题，导致训练和推理延迟过高，限制了其在实际应用中的效率。

Method: 提出CUDA加速的AC-MPC变体，利用GPU并行计算加速可微MPC层的优化求解过程，减少前向和反向传播的计算时间。

Result: 在无人机竞速任务中，该方法实现了最先进的单圈时间和接近极限的动态行为，同时显著降低了训练和推理时间。

Conclusion: CUDA加速有效解决了AC-MPC的计算瓶颈，在不牺牲控制性能的前提下大幅提升效率，为实时控制应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: In the literature, actor-critic model predictive control (AC-MPC) integrates MPC with reinforcement learning to enable high-performance control of complex dynamical systems. However, its differentiable MPC layer requires repeatedly solving an optimization problem in both the forward and backward passes, leading to substantial training and inference latency. This paper tackles this bottleneck introducing a CUDA-accelerated variant that significantly reduces end-to-end execution time while preserving the control performance of the baseline formulation. Simulation results on an agile drone racing task show that our approach achieves state-of-the-art lap times and near-limit dynamic behaviour with markedly reduced training and inference time.

</details>


### [143] [Extreme dynamic symmetry enables omnidirectional and multifunctional robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29254)
*Jiaxun Liu,Boxi Xia,Boyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出“动态对称性”概念，通过在机器人中实现质心加速度的各向同性（动态各向同性），大幅提升轨迹跟踪、任务成功率、鲁棒性、恢复力和能效。作者开发了Argus系列球形机器人，其中20腿实体变体实现了近极限动态各向同性，展现出方向无关运动、复杂地形穿越、快速自稳定和抗执行器故障等能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然界中对称性是核心组织原则，但机器人领域此前仅将其局限于几何形态设计。作者希望将对称性思想推广到机器人动态驱动能力层面，探索利用“动态对称性”统一提升多种性能指标的可行性。

Method: 提出动态对称性（dynamic symmetry）概念，并以动态各向同性（dynamic isotropy）作为量化指标。通过1000+模拟形态的系统研究验证其优势，进而设计Argus系列球形机器人——采用径向线性执行器直接塑造质心动力学，逐步提升动态对称性水平。最终制造了20腿近极限动态各向同性实体原型进行验证。

Result: 更高动态对称性在1000+模拟形态中一致改善了轨迹跟踪、任务成功、鲁棒性、恢复力和能效；20腿Argus实体实现了近极限动态各向同性，展示了方向无关运动、杂乱/可变形地形敏捷穿越、快速自稳定以及对部分执行器失效的容错能力，同时分布式感知支持连续运动中的全向感知与物体交互。

Conclusion: 不仅从形态上、更从可达动力学层面设计机器人的对称性，为在不确定陆地与地外环境中实现敏捷性、鲁棒性和多功能性提供了一条通用且强大的路径。

Abstract: Symmetry is a central organizing principle in natural systems, yet its use as a unifying design strategy in robotics has largely remained limited to geometric form. We show that symmetry can instead be leveraged at the level of dynamic actuation capability. We introduce dynamic symmetry, the uniformity of a robot's attainable center-of-mass accelerations, and formalize it through a measure coined as dynamic isotropy. Across more than 1000 simulated morphologies, we found that higher dynamic symmetry consistently improved trajectory tracking, task success, robustness, resiliency, and energy efficiency, with the benefits becoming most pronounced as dynamic isotropy approached its theoretical limit. To study this regime systematically, we developed Argus, a family of spherical robots designed to explore the effects of increasing dynamic symmetry. Members of the Argus family vary in their actuation geometry and dynamic symmetry level while sharing a common architectural principle: radially oriented linear actuators that directly shape the robot's center-of-mass dynamics. Among them, we built a physical 20-leg Argus variant that achieved near-extreme dynamic isotropy and demonstrated orientation-invariant locomotion, agile traversal of cluttered and deformable terrain, rapid self-stabilization, and resilience to partial actuator failures. Its distributed sensing further enabled omnidirectional perception and object interaction during continuous motion. These results show that designing robots for symmetry not only in morphology but also in their attainable dynamics provides a powerful and general pathway toward agility, robustness, and multifunctionality in uncertain terrestrial and extraterrestrial environments.

</details>


### [144] [MonoDuo: Using One Robot Arm to Learn Bimanual Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29298)
*Sandeep Bajamahal,Lawrence Yunliang Chen,Toru Lin,Zehan Ma,Jitendra Malik,Ken Goldberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MonoDuo提出了一种利用单臂机器人演示和人类协作来训练双臂机器人操作策略的方法。通过单臂遥操作+人类配合采集数据，再用合成演示训练双臂策略，在五个任务上实现了最高70%的零样本成功率，且小样本微调可进一步提升65-70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂机器人数据稀缺且昂贵，而单臂机器人广泛可用。作者希望利用单臂机器人资源来训练双臂操作策略，解决数据瓶颈问题。

Method: 1) 数据采集：单臂机器人遥操作完成一侧任务，人类协作完成另一侧，然后交换角色覆盖两侧。2) 数据增强：利用手部姿态估计、图像/点云分割和修复技术，将RGB-D观测合成目标双臂机器人的演示数据。3) 策略训练：用这些基于真实机器人运动学的合成演示训练双臂策略。

Result: 在箱体搬运、背包打包、布料折叠、夹克拉链和盘子交接五个任务上评估。相比仅用人类双臂视频的方法，MonoDuo实现了对未见双臂机器人配置的零样本部署，成功率最高达70%。仅需25个目标机器人演示进行小样本微调，成功率比从头训练提升65-70%。

Conclusion: MonoDuo验证了利用单臂机器人数据高效迁移到双臂机器人策略的可行性，为缓解双臂机器人数据稀缺问题提供了一种实用方案。

Abstract: Bimanual coordination is essential for many real-world manipulation tasks, yet learning bimanual robot policies is limited by the scarcity of bimanual robots and datasets. Single-arm robots, however, are widely available in research labs. Can we leverage them to train bimanual robot policies? We present MonoDuo, a framework for learning bimanual manipulation policies using single-arm robot demonstrations paired with human collaboration. MonoDuo collects data by teleoperating a single-arm robot to perform one side of a bimanual task while a human performs the other, then swapping roles to cover both sides. RGB-D observations from a wrist-mounted and fixed camera are augmented into synthetic demonstrations for target bimanual robots using state-of-the-art hand pose estimation, image and point cloud segmentation, and inpainting. These synthetic demonstrations, grounded in real robot kinematics, are used to train bimanual policies. We evaluate MonoDuo on five tasks: box lifting, backpack packing, cloth folding, jacket zipping, and plate handover. Compared to approaches relying solely on human bimanual videos, MonoDuo enables zero-shot deployment on unseen bimanual robot configurations, achieving success rates up to 70%. With only 25 target robot demonstrations, few-shot finetuning further boosts success rates by 65-70% over training from scratch, demonstrating MonoDuo's effectiveness in efficiently transferring knowledge from single-arm robot data to bimanual robot policies.

</details>


### [145] [The Open Motion Planning Library 2.0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29301)
*Weihang Guo,Theodoros Tyrovouzis,Emiliano Flores,Clayton W. Ramsey,Zachary K. Kingston,Ioan A. Şucan,Mark Moll,Lydia E. Kavraki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OMPL 2.0 是开源运动规划库的重大升级，引入硬件加速以实现实时运动规划，并集成现代AI研究工作流，同时回顾了近二十年来该库与运动规划领域的共同发展历程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对实时运动规划的需求以及现代AI研究与传统运动规划融合的趋势，对已有近二十年历史的OMPL库进行重大升级。

Method: 通过硬件加速（如GPU、FPGA）提升规划速度，同时设计接口以无缝对接AI工作流（如机器学习、深度学习框架）。

Result: OMPL 2.0 实现了实时运动规划能力，并增强了与现代AI生态系统的兼容性。

Conclusion: OMPL 2.0 标志着运动规划库从离线规划向实时、AI融合方向的重要演进。

Abstract: The Open Motion Planning Library (OMPL), first released in 2008, has become a cornerstone of the motion planning community, providing implementations of a wide range of state-of-the-art sampling-based algorithms. Over almost two decades of continuous development, we have steadily expanded the library with new planners, state spaces, and problem formulations. These additions range from asymptotically optimal and lazy planners to constrained motion planning and planning with temporal-logic goals. Building on this foundation, we introduce OMPL 2.0, a major evolution of the library that targets real-time motion planning through hardware acceleration and integrates seamlessly with modern AI research workflows. We also reflect on how OMPL and the field of motion planning have grown together over the years, and discuss the library's broader impact on the research community.

</details>


### [146] [Decentralized LLM-Driven Coordination of Acoustic Robots for Contactless Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29378)
*Yingying Wang,Narsimlu Kemsaram,Sriram Subramanian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向声学机器人的自然语言驱动作业框架，结合Whisper语音识别、LLM语义解析、JSON任务表示和分布式调度，实现了接触式物体搬运的语音指令到多机器人任务计划的转化。在TurtleBot3声学机器人平台上验证，顺序任务成功率96%，并行86%，协同70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究在LLM与分布式声学移动机器人结合方面尚属空白，而自然语言界面可简化非专家用户与多机器人系统的交互，声学操控又具备非接触式作业的优势，因此探索二者融合具有重要价值。

Method: 采用Whisper进行语音识别，LLM进行语义解析生成结构化JSON任务表示（包含机器人分配、时序依赖、空间约束与同步要求），再通过分布式调度器将任务分发到两台配备超声相控阵的TurtleBot3声学机器人上执行。

Result: 在顺序执行、并行运输、协同搬运三种场景下，任务成功率分别为96%、86%和70%，验证了从自然语言指令到分布式机器人动作转换的可行性。

Conclusion: 自然语言命令可有效转化为分布式声学机器人的非接触式操控动作，LLM驱动的自动化在分布式人机交互系统中具有广阔潜力。

Abstract: Natural language interfaces can simplify interaction with multi-robot systems, especially when non-expert users need to issue high-level commands. Acoustic manipulation using ultrasonic phased arrays also enables contactless object handling for applications such as healthcare, laboratory automation, and precision transport. However, combining large language models (LLMs) with distributed acoustic mobile robots remains underexplored. This paper presents a decentralized framework for natural language-driven coordination of acoustic robots for contactless object manipulation. The system converts spoken instructions into executable multi-robot task plans using Whisper-based speech recognition, LLM-based semantic parsing, structured JSON task representation, and distributed scheduling. The JSON schema encodes robot assignments, temporal dependencies, spatial constraints, and synchronization requirements for sequential, parallel, and synchronized execution. The system is implemented on two TurtleBot3-based acoustic robots, each equipped with an ultrasonic phased array for contactless object transport. Experiments were conducted in three scenarios: sequential execution, parallel multi-robot transport, and synchronized cooperative manipulation. The system achieved task success rates of 96 percent for sequential tasks, 86 percent for parallel execution, and 70 percent for synchronized collaborative transport. These results show that natural language commands can be transformed into distributed robot actions for contactless manipulation, highlighting the potential of LLM-driven automation for human-robot interaction in distributed robotic systems.

</details>


### [147] [Phase-Conditioned Imitation Learning with Autonomous Failure Recovery for Robust Deformable Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29407)
*Dayuan Chen,Kai Tang,Yukuan Zhang,Kazuhiro Kosuge,Yasuhisa Hirata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种相位条件化、力觉感知的机器人框架（PHASER），用于鲁棒的变形体操作。通过FiLM调节的ACT编码器和多模态相位预测器，实现了自主错误恢复，将T恤挂置成功率从56%提升到87%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模仿学习策略（如ACT）在推理时依赖马尔可夫假设，当视觉相似的观测需要矛盾的动作时会产生状态混叠，并且无法从执行失败中自主恢复。现有方法缺乏对任务相位变化的感知和力觉信息的利用。

Method: 提出PHASER框架：1) FiLM调节的ACT编码器，根据任务相位调制特征提取；2) 多模态相位预测器，融合视觉、力和姿态反馈实时估计相位；3) 混合阻抗控制器实现柔顺执行；4) 触觉遥操作接口用于力觉数据收集。

Result: 消融实验表明FiLM调制显著优于无条件基线和token级条件基线；t-SNE分析显示FiLM诱导出良好分离的相位特定特征表示。在双臂挂置和取下T恤任务上，闭环系统将挂置成功率从56%提高到87%。

Conclusion: 该工作证明相位条件化和力觉感知对于机器人自主错误恢复的有效性，为变形体操作中的闭环控制提供了新思路。

Abstract: This paper presents a phase-conditioned, force-aware framework for robust deformable object manipulation. Standard imitation learning policies such as Action Chunking with Transformers (ACT) rely on a Markovian assumption at inference, causing state aliasing when visually similar observations require contradictory actions and preventing autonomous recovery from execution failures. We address this with a closed-loop hierarchical architecture. A FiLM-conditioned ACT encoder modulates feature extraction based on the current task phase, enabling a single unified policy to produce phase-specific behaviors while sharing action dynamics across phases. A multi-modal phase predictor fusing visual, force, and pose feedback estimates the phase in real time, detecting contact failures that are invisible to vision alone and autonomously triggering recovery trajectories. The system is completed by a hybrid impedance controller for compliant execution and a haptic teleoperation interface for force-aware data collection. Ablation studies show that FiLM-based modulation significantly outperforms both unconditioned and token-level conditioned baselines, and t-SNE analysis confirms that FiLM induces well-separated, phase-specific feature representations. Validated on hanging and removing a T-shirt with dual arms, the closed-loop system improves the hanging success rate from 56\% to 87\% through autonomous error recovery. Code and videos: https://leledeyuan00.github.io/phaser/

</details>


### [148] [A Progress-Aware Leader-Follower Midair Docking System for Dual-Drone Aerial Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29410)
*Yifan Cai,Jan Ming Kevin Tan,Xiangqi Li,Chenzhe Jin,Narsimlu Kemsaram,Valerio Modugno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于小型无人机的空中对接平台，采用主从编队和被动磁吸机构，实现了轻量、可重复的空中对接，并集成了完整的软硬件栈用于基准评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型无人机空中对接对相对位姿控制精度要求高，且受限于推力与载荷，现有平台难以兼顾可靠性与可重复性，因此需要一种轻量化、可基准测试的对接平台。

Method: 双四旋翼主从编队+被动磁吸轻量化对接框架；进度感知任务管理器控制四阶段（接近、对齐、捕获、稳定）；集成ROS 2与Crazyflie/PX4接口及同步日志。

Result: 在仿真与真实实验中，通过编队误差、基线/偏航一致性、对接成功率、对接时间及失败模式统计等指标对平台进行了定量评估。

Conclusion: 该平台支持对接监督与同步策略的统计对比，为模块化空中协作与可重复空中操控提供了实用的测试平台。

Abstract: Reliable midair docking between small unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) is essential for modular aerial cooperation and manipulation, but it requires precise relative-pose control and repeatable platform under tight thrust and payload constraints. We present a dual-drone docking platform where two quadrotors operate in a leader-follower formation and dock using a lightweight modular frame with passive magnetic latching. A progress-aware mission supervisor manages phase transitions: approach, alignment, capture, and settle. This platform integrates a complete hardware-software stack (ROS 2 with Crazyflie/PX4 interfaces) and synchronized logging for benchmark evaluation. We evaluate the platform in simulation and real-world experiments using quantitative metrics such as formation error, baseline and yaw consistency, docking success rate, time-to-dock, and failure-mode statistics. The platform enables statistically grounded comparison of docking supervision and synchronization strategies and provides a practical testbed for modular aerial cooperation and repeatable midair aerial manipulation.

</details>


### [149] [3DVLA: Enhancing Vision-Language-Action Models via 3D Spatial and Instance Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29416)
*Zhongyu Xia,Yousen Tang,Bingqing Wei,Yongtao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 3DVLA是一个即插即用的框架，通过多视角一致性约束、实例估计模块和自监督3D编码，为预训练VLA模型注入3D推理能力，在LIBERO-Plus和RoboTwin 2.0上显著提升操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型缺乏3D场景理解能力，表现为三点问题：无法提取具有多视角一致性的3D空间位置、缺乏3D实例理解、遮挡下推理脆弱。同时，成熟3D感知方法难以直接集成到VLA中，存在架构不兼容且依赖昂贵实例级标注的问题。

Method: 提出3DVLA框架：(1) 采用显式多视角一致性约束的3D特征编码和空间条件几何聚合方法；(2) 实例估计模块通过高层级实例token实现3D实例感知；(3) 掩码自监督3D编码分支保留预测器进行视觉token补全以处理遮挡。

Result: 在LIBERO-Plus和RoboTwin 2.0数据集上，3DVLA与多个VLA基线集成，在操作性能上取得一致且显著提升，验证了方法的有效性及即插即用兼容性。

Conclusion: 3DVLA无需额外人工标注或丢弃VLM先验知识，即可为预训练VLA注入鲁棒的3D推理能力，解决了3D场景理解缺失的关键限制。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models have achieved remarkable progress in robotic manipulation, yet they suffer from a critical limitation: a lack of 3D scene understanding. This deficiency manifests as three intertwined challenges: weak extraction of 3D spatial positions without enforcing multi-view consistency, inadequate 3D instance understanding, and fragile reasoning under occlusion. Although mature 3D perception methods exist, their direct integration into VLA pipelines is hindered by architectural incompatibility and by heavy reliance on costly instance-level annotations. To address the above challenges, we propose 3DVLA, a plug-and-play framework that injects robust 3D reasoning into pretrained VLAs without requiring extra manual labels or discarding VLM priors. Specifically, 3DVLA tackles the three challenges through: (1) pervasive 3D feature encoding with explicit multi-view consistency constraints across all modalities and a Spatially-Conditioned Geometry Aggregation method, (2) an instance estimation module with high-level instance tokens for 3D instance awareness, and (3) a masked self-supervised 3D encoding branch that retains its predictor for visual token completion to handle occlusions. We integrate 3DVLA with multiple VLA baselines and evaluate on LIBERO-Plus and RoboTwin 2.0. Results show consistent and significant gains in manipulation performance, validating both the effectiveness and plug-and-play compatibility of our approach.

</details>


### [150] [ElegantVLA: Learning When to Think for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29438)
*Ye Li,Huanan Liu,Kangye Ji,Yuan Meng,Jiajun Fan,Yuansong Wang,Shiyu Qin,Chenglei Wu,Shu-Tao Xia,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ElegantVLA，一种即插即用的相位自适应推理框架，通过动态计算调度（在视觉编码器、LLM和动作头之间分配计算资源）加速VLA机器人控制模型，在保持性能的同时实现2.18-3.77倍加速，将控制频率从13.8Hz提升至26.3Hz。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型计算成本高、控制频率低，且现有加速方法忽略顺序控制中不同控制步的非均匀推理需求，因此需要一种能根据推理阶段动态分配计算资源的加速框架。

Method: 提出ElegantVLA框架：(1) 轻量级调度器，根据时序表征相似性、机器人运动线索和任务进度，动态分配计算；(2) 感知-语言推理阶段：5级Vision-LLM计算模式（从完整重计算到多步时序复用）；(3) 动作生成阶段：3级去噪模式（稳定运动时复用中间去噪状态，目标敏感阶段保留完整精化）。

Result: 在GR00T和CogACT上分别实现2.55倍和3.77倍加速；在6个真实世界GR00T任务中，计算量减少2.18倍，控制频率从13.8Hz提升至26.3Hz。

Conclusion: 受人类运动控制启发（认知和反馈资源集中在目标敏感阶段），证明VLA模型应学习何时投入完整计算、何时复用先前计算，ElegantVLA提供了一种无需修改或重训练基础模型的通用加速框架。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are a powerful paradigm for generalist robotic control.
